Tuple\nimport numpy as np\nimport os\nimport sys\n\n# Import SEED from config if available\n_config_seed = None\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\nfrom config import SEED as _config_seed\n\n\ndef evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    \n    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n        # MetaBox style: use problem.func(x), which REQUIRES 2D input [NP, dim]\n        if x.ndim == 1:\n            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n        result = problem.func(x)\n        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    else:\n        # cocoex style: problem(x)\n        return float(problem(x))\n\n\nclass DEOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    \n    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: int,\n                 params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, controller=None, recorder=None,\n                 swarm_size: int = None):\n        self.dim = int(dim)\n        if lower_bounds is not None:\n            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.lower_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.lower_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.lower_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.lower_bounds = -5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        if upper_bounds is not None:\n            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.upper_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.upper_bounds = 5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n        self.controller = controller\n        self.recorder = recorder\n        self.stats = {}\n        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n\n    def _reflect_boundary(self, x, lb, ub):\n        \"\"\"Reflect out-of-bound coordinates back into [lb, ub].\"\"\"\n        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).copy()\n        for _ in range(10):\n            changed = False\n            for j in range(x.shape[0]):\n                if x[j] < lb[j]:\n                    x[j] = 2.0 * lb[j] - x[j]\n                    changed = True\n                elif x[j] > ub[j]:\n                    x[j] = 2.0 * ub[j] - x[j]\n                    changed = True\n            if not changed:\n                break\n        return np.clip(x, lb, ub)\n\n    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n        selected = []\n        for _ in range(tournsize):\n            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n            selected.append(idx)\n        best_idx = selected[0]\n        for idx in selected[1:]:\n            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n                best_idx = idx\n        return best_idx\n\n    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        if self.controller is not None:\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\n        \n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n        \n        # Evaluate initial population\n        evals = 0\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n        \n        for i in range(pop_size):\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                break\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n            evals += 1\n\n        # Track best\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n        \n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n\n        # JADE archives and success-history parameters\n        archive = []\n        archive_max = max(1, int(pop_size * 2.0))\n        mu_F = float(self.F)\n        mu_CR = float(self.CR)\n        c = 0.1\n\n        # Main loop\n        iteration = 0\n        done = False\n        \n        while not done:\n            iteration += 1\n            params = dict(self.params)\n            if self.controller is not None:\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n            \n            # Base F/CR from controller or defaults, blended with success-history means\n            base_F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n            base_CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n            if mu_F > 0:\n                base_F = 0.5 * base_F + 0.5 * mu_F\n            if mu_CR > 0:\n                base_CR = 0.5 * base_CR + 0.5 * mu_CR\n            base_F = min(1.0, max(0.1, base_F))\n            base_CR = min(1.0, max(0.0, base_CR))\n            \n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n            \n            # Generate trial vectors using JADE-style current-to-pbest/1 with archive\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n            trial_f = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n            success_F = []\n            success_CR = []\n\n            # pbest selection pool (top p% of the population)\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n            progress_frac = evals / max(1, self.max_evals)\n            pbest_ratio = max(0.05, 0.25 * (1.0 - progress_frac))\n            n_pbest = max(2, int(pop_size * pbest_ratio))\n\n            # Archive pool (optional archive vectors used in mutation)\n            if len(archive) > 0:\n                archive_pool = np.vstack([pop] + archive)\n            else:\n                archive_pool = pop\n            n_arch = len(archive_pool)\n\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # Sample individual F_i and CR_i around the adaptively updated means\n                F_i = base_F + 0.1 * rng.randn()\n                F_i = min(1.0, max(0.1, F_i))\n                CR_i = base_CR + 0.1 * rng.randn()\n                CR_i = min(1.0, max(0.0, CR_i))\n\n                # Base vector from the pbest subset (exploitation / convergence)\n                pbest_idx = int(sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, n_pbest)])\n                x_pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\n\n                # Two distinct random vectors from the population, excluding i\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n                r1, r2 = rng.choice(candidates, size=2, replace=False)\n\n                # DE/current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (x_pbest - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[r1] - pop[r2])\n\n                # With 10% probability use an archive vector as the second difference term\n                if len(archive) > 0 and rng.random() < 0.1:\n                    arch_idx = int(rng.randint(0, n_arch))\n                    mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (x_pbest - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[r1] - archive_pool[arch_idx])\n\n                # Binomial crossover\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand:\n                        new_pop[i, j] = mutant[j]\n                    else:\n                        new_pop[i, j] = pop[i, j]\n\n                # Boundary handling: reflect then clip (preserves diversity better than clipping)\n                new_pop[i] = self._reflect_boundary(new_pop[i], lb, ub)\n\n            # Evaluate and select\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n                trial_f[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n\n                if trial_f[i] < fitness[i]:\n                    # Add old parent to the archive (FIFO with bounded size)\n                    if len(archive) < archive_max:\n                        archive.append(pop[i].copy())\n                    else:\n                        archive[int(rng.randint(0, len(archive)))] = pop[i].copy()\n\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f[i]\n                    success_F.append(F_i)\n                    success_CR.append(CR_i)\n                    if trial_f[i] < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f[i]\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n\n            # Update success-history means (Lehmer mean for F, arithmetic mean for CR)\n            c = 0.1\n            if len(success_F) > 0:\n                sum_sq = sum(f * f for f in success_F)\n                sum_f = sum(success_F)\n                if sum_f > 1e-12:\n                    mu_F = (1.0 - c) * mu_F + c * (sum_sq / sum_f)\n                else:\n                    mu_F = (1.0 - c) * mu_F\n                mu_CR = (1.0 - c) * mu_CR + c * (sum(success_CR) / len(success_CR))\n                mu_F = min(1.0, max(0.1, mu_F))\n                mu_CR = min(1.0, max(0.0, mu_CR))\n            else:\n                mu_F = (1.0 - c) * mu_F\n                mu_CR = (1.0 - c) * mu_CR\n\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n            \n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n            if 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n            \n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n\n        self.stats = {\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n            'evals': int(evals),\n            'iterations': F_i))\n                CR_i = base_CR + 0.1 * rng.randn()\n                CR_i = min(1.0, max(0.0, CR_i))\n\n                # Base vector from the pbest subset (exploitation / convergence)\n                pbest_idx = int(sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, n_pbest)])\n                x_pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\n\n                # Two distinct random vectors from the population, excluding i\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n                r1, r2 = rng.choice(candidates, size=2, replace=False)\n\n                # DE/current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (x_pbest - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[r1] - pop[r2])\n\n                # With 10% probability use an archive vector as the second difference term\n                if len(archive) > 0 and rng.random() < 0.1:\n                    arch_idx = int(rng.randint(0, n_arch))\n                    mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (x_pbest - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[r1] - archive_pool[arch_idx])\n\n                # Binomial crossover\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand:\n than clipping)\n                new_pop[i] = self._reflect_boundary(new_pop[i], lb, ub)\n\n            # Evaluate and select\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n                trial_f[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n\n                if trial_f[i] < fitness[i]:\n                    # Add old parent to the archive (FIFO with bounded size)\n                    if len(archive) < archive_max:\n                        archive.append(pop[i].copy())\n                    else:\n                        archive[int(rng.randint(0, len(archive)))] = pop[i].copy()\n\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f[i]\n                    success_F.append(F_i)\n                    success_CR.append(CR_i)\n                    if trial_f[i] < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f[i]\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n\n            # Update success-history means (Lehmer mean for F, arithmetic mean for CR)\n            c = 0.1\n            if len(success_F) > 0:\n                sum_sq = sum(f * f for f in success_F)\n                sum_f = sum(success_F)\n                if sum_f > 1e-12:\n                    mu_F = (1.0 - c) * mu_F + c
